The board (the “Board”) of directors (the “Directors”) of Hao       昊天國際建設投資集團有限公司（「本公司」）董事
Tian International Construction Investment Group Limited (the       （「董事」）會（「董事會」）欣然向本公司股東（「股東」）
“Company”) is pleased to present the shareholders of the            呈 列 本 公 司 及 其 附 屬 公 司（「本 集 團」）截 至 二 零
Company (the “Shareholders”) the annual results of the              二二年三月三十一日止年度（「本年度」）之年度業
Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) for the year ended       績。
31 March 2022 (the “Year”).


During the Year, economies have been through challenges both        於本年度，環球經濟和香港經濟仍充滿挑戰。年
globally and in Hong Kong. As the COVID-19 pandemic was still       內世界各地仍受 COVID-19 疫情影響，宏觀環境不
threatening the whole world in the Year, the macro environment      容 樂 觀。 於 二 零 二 二 年 第 一 季 度， 香 港 的
was not optimistic. Given the hit record highs of the COVID-19      COVID-19 疫情屢創新高，重創各行各業導致經濟
pandemic in Hong Kong in 1st quarter of 2022, various sectors       衰退、香港的失業率更上升至二十年來高位。面
were heavily affected and thus led to economic recession and        對錯綜複雜的外部環境，本集團採取穩健財務措
the highest unemployment rate in the last two decades. In view      施，並積極探尋新的發展機遇。
of the complicated external environment, the Group has
adopted a sound financial policy, and actively explored new
development opportunities.


In terms of the existing construction machinery business, due to    就現有建築機械業務而言，於本年度，由於承接
the relatively stable undertaking of government infrastructure      政府基建項目情況相對穩定，故本集團 (i) 建築機
projects, the Group managed to record an increase in the (i)        械及備用零件銷售額，及 (ii) 建築機械產生之租金
sales of construction machinery and spare parts, and (ii) rental    收入均錄得增幅，租賃機械佔用率約為 85%。
income generated from construction machinery during the Year.
The occupancy rate for the rental machineries was
approximately 85%.


For the financial services business, we shall continue our          就金融服務業務而言，我們將以謹慎積極態度繼
business expansion with a cautious and positive attitude. The       續擴充業務。本集團對香港及中國內地金融市場
Group is confident in the prospects of the financial markets in     前景充滿信心，並有意透過內生增長或與聲譽良
Hong Kong and the Mainland China, and would like to increase        好的夥伴合作，壯大本集團於金融市場的版圖。

Impact of COVID-19                                                     COVID-19 之影響

During the Year under review, the COVID-19 pandemic                    於本回顧年度，COVID-19 疫情因變異病毒株的爆
remained challenging due to the outbreak of the mutant                 發及於香港爆發的第 5 波 COVID-19 疫情依然具挑
variants and outbreak of 5th wave of COVID-19 in Hong Kong.            戰性。本集團繼續維持穩定及專注於現有業務。
The Group remained resilient and focused on its existing
businesses.


Though operations of the construction industry in Hong Kong            儘管香港建造業涉及的業務受到 COVID-19 之後續
have been affected as a consequence of COVID-19, due to the            影響，惟由於承接政府基建項目情況相對穩定，
relatively stable undertaking of government infrastructure             於本年度本集團 (i) 建築機械及備用零件銷售額，
projects, the Group managed to record an increase in the (i)           及 (ii) 建築機械產生之租金收入均錄得增幅。租賃
sales of construction machinery and spare parts, and (ii) rental       機械佔用率保持在約 85%。
income generated from construction machinery during the Year.
The occupancy rate for the rental machineries kept at
approximately 85%.


To manage the impact of COVID-19, the Group maintained its             為管理 COVID-19 帶來之影響，本集團維持其營運
operation and put in place precautionary and risk mitigation           並採取預防及降低風險之措施，當中包括適當作
measures, including suitably deploying remote work                     出遙距工作安排，定期徹底清潔與消毒倉庫及辦
arrangements, periodic deep cleaning and sanitization for both         公室，為全體僱員提供口罩或其他個人保護設備，
our warehouse and offices, provision of facemasks or other             僱員每天進行 COVID-19 抗原測試及為工作場所內
personal protection equipment for all employees, daily                 全體人員量度體溫以及妥善記錄工作時間表及接
COVID-19 Antigen Test and body temperature measurement                 觸記錄，以便追蹤。
for all personnel at workplaces, and proper record keeping of
work schedule and contact history for tracing.


During the Year, the Group continued to pursue a long-term             於本年度，本集團繼續奉行長遠業務策略，多元
business strategy of diversifying into financial services business,    化拓展至金融服務業務、物業發展業務及建築機
property development business, and construction machinery              械業務。本集團主要業務包括：(i) 放貸；(ii) 證券
business. The Group’s principal activities include: (i) money          投資；(iii) 提供商品、期貨、證券經紀以及金融服
lending; (ii) securities investment; (iii) provision of commodities,   務；(iv) 資產管理；(v) 建築機械租賃及銷售；及 (vi)
futures, securities brokerage and financial services; (iv) asset       物業發展。
management; (v) rental and trading of construction machinery;
and (vi) property development.

different classes of receivables according to their respective risk   時考慮之因素包括但不限於應收賬款之賬齡分析、
characteristics. In determining the default risk, factors including   本集團對債務人信用狀況之內部評估、發生違約
but not limited to, the ageing analysis of the receivables, the       事件之歷史及預測、抵押品之存在及估值、香港

Interest rate risk                                                     利率風險

The Group’s pledged bank deposits and finance lease                    本集團之已抵押銀行存款及融資租賃應收款項以
receivables bear fixed interest rates. The Group’s cash at bank        固定利率計息。本集團之銀行現金結餘以浮動利
balances bear floating interest rates. The Group also has              率計息。本集團亦有借貸及融資租賃責任，及來
borrowings and obligation under finance leases and a loan from         自一名董事之貸款部分以固定利率計算利息及部
a director which is partly bearing interests at fixed interest rates   分免息。倘有未能預料的不利利率變動，該等以
and partly interest free. Exposure to interest rate risk exists on     浮動利率計息之結餘將面對利率風險。本集團的
those balances subject to floating interest rate when there are        政策為在協定之框架內管理其利率風險，以確保
unexpected adverse interest rate movements. The Group’s                不會面對利率大幅變動此不合理風險，並於有需
policy is to manage its interest rate risk, working within an          要時適當地固定利率。
agreed framework, to ensure that there are no undue exposures
to significant interest rate movements and rates are
appropriately fixed when necessary.


Currency risk                                                          貨幣風險

The Group mainly operates in Hong Kong with most of the                本集團於香港營業，大部分交易以港元、美元、
transactions denominated and settled in HK$, US$, JPY and              日元及歐元計值及結算。本集團面對的外幣風險
EUR. The Group’s exposure to foreign currency risk primarily           主要來自以美元及歐元計值之若干金融工具，包
arises from certain financial instruments including trade              括貿易應收款項、銀行結餘及現金、貿易應付款
receivables, bank balances and cash, trade payables,                   項、借貸及融資租賃責任。本集團並無採納任何
borrowings and obligation under finance leases which are               長遠對沖策略，但管理層持續監察外匯風險並可
denominated in US$ and EUR. The Group does not adopt any               能按個別情況訂立遠期匯兌合約。本集團並無採
hedging strategy in the long run but the management                    用任何對沖合約以從事投機活動。
continuously monitors the foreign exchange risk exposure and
might enter into foreign exchange forward contracts on a case-
by-case basis. The Group has not used any hedging contracts
to engage in speculative activities.


Credit risk and liquidity risk                                         信貸風險及流動資金風險

The Group has adopted a prudent financial management                   本集團的庫務政策已採取審慎的財務管理方針，
approach towards its treasury policies and thus maintained a           故已在本年度維持穩健的流動資金狀況。本集團
healthy liquidity position throughout the Year. The Group strives      致力透過進行持續的信貸評估及判斷其客戶的財
to reduce exposure to credit risk by performing ongoing credit         務狀況降低信貸風險。為管理流動資金風險，董
assessments and evaluations of the financial status of its             事會密切監察本集團的流動資金狀況，以確保本
customers. To manage liquidity risk, the Board closely monitors        集團的資產、負債及其他承擔的流動資金架構符
the Group’s liquidity position to ensure that the liquidity            合其資金要求。
structure of the Group’s assets, liabilities and other
commitments can meet its funding requirements.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT
企業管治報告

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                        企業管治常規

The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and                本公司及董事會致力達致及維持最高水平之企業

Summary of the Board Diversity Policy                                 董事會多元化政策摘要

With a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced                   為達致可持續均衡發展，本公司視董事會層面日
development, the Company sees increasing diversity at the             益多元化為支持其達到策略目標及可持續發展之
Board level as an essential element in supporting the attainment      關鍵元素。本公司在謀劃董事會成員組合時，已
of its strategic objectives and its sustainable development. In       從多方面考慮董事會多元化，包括但不限於性別、
designing the Board’s composition, the diversity of the Board         年齡、文化及教育背景、專業經驗、技能以及知
has been considered from a number of aspects, including but           識。董事會所有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並在
not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational                  考慮人選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會多元化之
background, professional experience, skills and knowledge. All        裨益。
Board appointments will be based on merit, and candidates will
be considered against objective criteria, having due regard for
the benefits of diversity on the Board.


Measurable Objectives                                                 可計量目標

Selection of candidates will be based on a range of diversified       甄選人選將按一系列多元化範疇為基準，包括但
perspectives, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural      不限於性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、專業經驗、
and educational background, professional experience, skills and       技能及知識。最終將按人選之長處及可為董事會
knowledge. The ultimate decision will be based on merit and           作出之潛在貢獻作決定。
potential contribution that the selected candidates will bring to
the Board.

accounting or legal with extensive experience.

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                      股息政策

The Company has adopted a dividend policy. The Board would           本公司已採納股息政策。於宣派或建議向股東派
consider, inter alia, the following factors before declaring or      付股息前，董事會將考慮（其中包括）下列因素：
recommending dividend to the Shareholders:


•    the Company’s actual and expected financial performance;        •     本公司之實際及預期財務表現；


•    retained earnings and distributable reserves of the             •     本公司之保留盈利及可供分派儲備；
     Company;


•    the level of the Company’s debts to equity ratio, return on     •     本公司之債務對權益比率水平、權益回報及
     equity and the relevant financial covenants;                          相關財務契諾；


•    any restrictions on payment of dividends that may be            •     本公司或其附屬公司所訂立任何合約可能對
     imposed by any contract entered into by the Company or                派付股息施加之任何限制；
     its subsidiaries;

PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                      主要業務

The Company is an investment holding company and the Group                本公司為投資控股公司，本集團則主要從事以下
is principally engaged in the following principal activities:             業務：


(a)     the construction machinery business, serving primarily the        (a)   建築機械業務，主要服務於香港之建築行
        construction sector in Hong Kong, including (i) rental of               業，當中包括 (i) 建築機械租賃；(ii) 建築機械
        construction machinery; (ii) trading of construction                    及備用零件銷售；及 (iii) 提供機械運輸服務；
        machinery and spare parts; and (iii) provision of machinery
        transportation services;


(b)     the provision of financial services, conducting: (i) Type 1       (b)   提供金融服務，進行：(i) 證券及期貨條例項
        (dealing in securities), Type 2 (dealing in futures contracts),         下 第 1 類（證 券 交 易）、第 2 類（期 貨 合 約 交
        Type 4 (advising on securities) and Type 9 (asset                       易）、第 4 類（就證券提供意見）及第 9 類（提
        management) regulated activities under the Securities and               供資產管理）受規管活動；及 (ii) 放貸業務；

PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                    主要風險及不明朗因素

The Group identified and determined the major risks which may        本集團通過風險管理流程識別並確定可能影響其
affect the operation results and financial conditions of the         經營業績及財務狀況之主要風險如下：
Group through risk management process, which includes the
following:


Risk in delay or default of payment in the Group’s                   本集團放貸業務之延遲或拖欠還款風險
money lending business

As at 31 March 2022, the outstanding loan receivables of the         於二零二二年三月三十一日，本集團放貸業務未
money lending business of the Group (net of impairment) was          償還應收貸款（扣除減值）約為 59 百萬港元，佔本
approximately HK$59 million, representing approximately 2% of        集團資產總值約 2%。任何延遲或拖欠貸款還款均
the total assets of the Group. Any delay or default in payment       會對本公司營運資金、現金流量及溢利造成不利
of the loans will have an adverse impact on the Company’s            影響。
working capital, cash flow and subsequently the profits of the
Company.


To manage such risk, the Company requires borrowers to               為管理該等風險，本公司要求借款人提供適當之
provide appropriate security and closely monitors the loan           抵押品，並不時密切監控應收貸款之收款週期，
receivable collection cycle from time to time to get full            以悉數收回應收借款人之未償還款項。本公司將
collection of the outstanding amounts due from borrowers. The        評估及考慮執行對借款人所提供之抵押品之權利
Company will assess and decide to enforce the security               或採取法律行動（如適用）。所有風險敞口均須定
provided by borrowers or take legal actions as appropriate. All      期檢討及評估減值之可能性。
exposures are subject to regular review and assessment for
possible impairment.

subjected to regulatory proceedings, disciplinary action, or loss    究法律責任、採取監管措施、給予紀律處分、出
of property or business reputation arising from violation of laws,   現財產損失或商業信譽損失之風險。為有效監控

value of the Group by motivating the participants to strive for        東利益相符，從而實現提升本集團價值此目標。
the continual operation and future development and expansion
of the Group and aligning the interests of the participants and

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE AUDIT                             核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責任
OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL
STATEMENTS

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about              我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在
whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole are         由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理
free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error,      保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們
and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We       僅向     閣下（作為整體）報告我們的意見，除此之
report our opinion solely to you, as a body, and for no other        外別無其他目的。我們並不就本報告的內容對任
purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept           何其他人士承擔任何責任或接受任何義務。合理
liability to any other person for the contents of this report.       保證是高水準的保證，但不能保證按照香港審計
Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a      準則進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs           能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.           合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered       報表使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could            則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of
users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial
statements.

2.   APPLICATION OF NEW AND REVISED HONG                             2.   採納新訂及經修訂香港財務報告準
     KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING STANDARDS                                   則

     In the current year, the Group has adopted all the new and           於本年度，本集團已採納由香港會計師公會

     Reporting Standards (“HKFRS”); Hong Kong Accounting                  則」）；香港會計準則（「香港會計準則」）；及
     Standards (“HKAS”); and Interpretations. The adoption of             詮釋。採納該等新訂及經修訂香港財務報告
     these new and revised HKFRSs did not result in significant           準則並無導致於本年度及過往年度本集團之

     to 31 March. Subsidiaries are entities over which the                     團擁有控制權之實體。當本集團參與實體業
     Group has control. The Group controls an entity when it is                務而對其可變回報承擔風險或享有可變回報
     exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                      之權利，且能夠通過其對該實體之權力影響

     activities, i.e. activities that significantly affect the entity’s
     returns.


     When assessing control, the Group considers its potential                 在評估控制權時，本集團會考慮其潛在投票
     voting rights as well as potential voting rights held by                  權以及其他人士持有之潛在投票權，以釐定
     other parties, to determine whether it has control. A                     其是否擁有控制權。在持有人能實際行使潛
     potential voting right is considered only if the holder has               在投票權之情況下，方會考慮其權利。
     the practical ability to exercise that right.

     control is transferred to the Group. They are                             入賬，並自控制權終止當日起終止綜合入賬。
     deconsolidated from the date the control ceases.

     in a loss of control represents the difference between (i)                益或虧損指以下兩項之差額：(i) 銷售代價之
     the fair value of the consideration of the sale plus the fair             公平值加上該附屬公司所保留之任何投資之
     value of any investment retained in that subsidiary and (ii)              公平值，及 (ii) 本公司分佔該附屬公司資產淨
     the Company’s share of the net assets of that subsidiary                  值加上與該附屬公司有關之任何剩餘商譽及
     plus any remaining goodwill relating to that subsidiary and               任何相關之累計外幣換算儲備。
     any related accumulated foreign currency translation
     reserve.

     Associates                                                             聯營公司

     Associates are entities over which the Group has                       聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力之實
     significant influence. Significant influence is the power to           體。重大影響力乃於有關實體之財務及營運
     participate in the financial and operating policy decisions            政策之決策擁有參與權而非控制或共同控制
     of an entity but is not control or joint control over those            該等政策。包括其他實體持有之潛在投票權
     policies. The existence and effect of potential voting rights          在內之現時可行使或可兌換之潛在投票權之
     that are currently exercisable or convertible, including               存在及影響將於評估本集團是否擁有重大影
     potential voting rights held by other entities, are                    響力時予以考慮。在評估一項潛在投票權有
     considered when assessing whether the Group has                        否重大影響力時，不會考慮持有者是否有意
     significant influence. In assessing whether a potential                及在財務上是否有能力行使或兌換該項權利。
     voting right contributes to significant influence, the
     holder’s intention and financial ability to exercise or
     convert that right is not considered.


     Investment in an associate is accounted for in the                     於聯營公司之投資乃採用權益法於綜合財務
     consolidated financial statements by the equity method                 報表列賬，並按成本作出初始確認。所收購
     and is initially recognised at cost. Identifiable assets and           聯營公司之可識別資產及負債乃按其於收購
     liabilities of the associate in an acquisition are measured at         當日之公平值計算。倘收購成本超出本集團
     their fair values at the acquisition date. The excess of the           分佔聯營公司之可識別資產及負債之公平值
     cost of acquisition over the Group’s share of the net fair             淨值，則差額將以商譽列賬。該商譽將列入
     value of the associate’s identifiable assets and liabilities is        投資之賬面值，並在有客觀證據顯示投資出
     recorded as goodwill. The goodwill is included in the                  現減值時連同於各報告期末之投資一併進行
     carrying amount of the investment and is tested for                    減值測試。倘本集團分佔可識別資產及負債
     impairment together with the investment at the end of                  之公平值淨值高於收購成本，則有關差額將
     each reporting period when there is objective evidence                 於綜合損益內確認。
     that the investment is impaired. Any excess of the Group’s
     share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and
     liabilities over the cost of acquisition is recognised in
     consolidated profit or loss.

     Joint arrangements                                                     共同安排

     A joint arrangement is an arrangement of which two or                  合營安排指雙方或多方於其中擁有共同控制
     more parties have joint control. Joint control is the                  權之安排。共同控制是指按照合約約定對某
     contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,             項安排所共有之控制，共同控制僅在當相關
     which exists only when decisions about the relevant                    活動要求共同享有控制權之各方作出一致同
     activities require the unanimous consent of the parties                意之決定時存在。相關活動指對安排回報有
     sharing control. Relevant activities are activities that               重大影響之活動。在評估共同控制時，本集
     significantly affect the returns of the arrangement. When              團會考慮其潛在投票權，以及由其他方持有
     assessing joint control, the Group considers its potential             之潛在投票權，以釐定其是否具有共同控制
     voting rights as well as potential voting rights held by               權。潛在投票權只有在持有人具有行使該權
     other parties, to determine whether it has joint control. A            利之實際能力時才予以考慮。
     potential voting right is considered only if the holder has
     the practical ability to exercise that right.


     A joint arrangement is either a joint operation or a joint             合營安排指共同經營或合營企業。共同經營
     venture. A joint operation is a joint arrangement whereby              乃對該項安排擁有共同控制權之各方對該項
     the parties that have joint control of the arrangement have            安排之資產擁有權利及對其負債承擔責任之
     rights to the assets, and obligations for the liabilities,             合營安排。合營企業乃擁有共同控制權之各
     relating to the arrangement. A joint venture is a joint                方對該項安排之淨資產擁有權利之合營安排。
     arrangement whereby the parties that have joint control of
     the arrangement have rights to the net assets of the
     arrangement.


     In relation to its interest in a joint operation, the Group            有關共同經營之權益，本集團乃按照適用於
     recognises in its consolidated financial statements, its               特定資產、負債、收益及開支之香港財務報
     assets, including its share of any assets held jointly; its            告準則，於其綜合財務報表中確認其資產（包
     liabilities, including its share of any liabilities incurred           括分佔共同持有之任何資產）；其負債（包括
     jointly; its revenue from the sale of its share of the output          分佔共同產生之任何負債）；來自出售其共
     arising from the joint operation; its share of the revenue             同經營所分佔產出之收益；其在共同經營中
     from the sale of the output by the joint operation; and its            產出所分佔之收益；及其開支（包括分佔共
     expenses, including its share of any expenses incurred                 同產生之任何開支）。
     jointly, in accordance with the HKFRSs applicable to the
     particular assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses.

     Joint arrangements (continued)                                        共同安排（續）

     Investment in a joint venture is accounted for in the                 於合營企業之投資乃按權益法記入綜合財務

     and is initially recognised at cost. Identifiable assets and          之可識別資產及負債按收購日期之公平值計

     end of each reporting period when there is objective

     specified in the relevant contract is discharged, cancelled              期，則金融負債將被終止確認。終止確認之
     or expires. The difference between the carrying amount of                金融負債之賬面值與已付代價之差額於損益
     the financial liability derecognised and the consideration               內確認。
     paid is recognised in profit or loss.

     Loss allowances for expected credit losses                             預期信貸虧損之虧損撥備

     The Group recognises loss allowances for expected credit               本集團按攤銷成本及融資租賃應收款項確認
     losses on financial assets at amortised cost and finance               金融資產之預期信貸虧損之虧損撥備。預期
     lease receivables. Expected credit losses are the weighted             信貸虧損乃加權平均信貸虧損，並以發生相
     average of credit losses with the respective risks of a                關違約之風險作為加權數值。
     default occurring as the weights.


     At the end of each reporting period, the Group measures                於各報告期末或倘金融工具之信貸風險自初
     the loss allowance for a financial instrument at an amount             始確認以來大幅增加，本集團就貿易應收款
     equal to the expected credit losses that result from all               項，按相等於所有可能發生違約事件之預期
     possible default events over the expected life of that                 信貸虧損除以該金融工具之預計年期（「全期
     financial instrument (“lifetime expected credit losses”) for           預期信貸虧損」）之金額，計量金融工具之虧
     trade receivables or if the credit risk on that financial              損撥備。
     instrument has increased significantly since initial
     recognition.

     (continued)

     Revenue from contracts with customers                               客戶合約收入

     Revenue is measured based on the consideration specified            收入參考常見業務慣例按客戶合約列明之代
     in a contract with a customer with reference to the                 價計量，且不包括代表第三方收取之款項。
     customary business practices and excludes amounts                   倘合約規定客戶付款與轉移承諾產品或服務
     collected on behalf of third parties. For a contract where          之間之期間超過一年，代價須就重大融資組
     the period between the payment by the customer and the              成部分之影響作出調整。
     transfer of the promised product or service exceeds one
     year, the consideration is adjusted for the effect of a
     significant financing component.


     The Group recognises revenue when it satisfies a                    本集團於透過將某項產品或服務之控制權轉
     performance obligation by transferring control over a               移至客戶而達成履約責任時確認收入。視乎
     product or service to a customer. Depending on the terms            合約條款及該合約適用之法例，履約責任可
     of a contract and the laws that apply to that contract, a           隨時間或於特定時間點達成。倘符合以下條
     performance obligation can be satisfied over time or at a           件，履約責任須隨時間達成：
     point in time. A performance obligation is satisfied over
     time if:


     —     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes             —     客戶於本集團履約時同時接受及使用
           the benefits provided by the Group’s performance;                   本集團履約所提供之利益；


     —     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an                —     本集團履約產生或增強一項於產生或
           asset that the customer controls as the asset is                    增強時由客戶控制之資產；或
           created or enhanced; or


     —     the Group’s performance does not create an asset              —     本集團履約並無產生對本集團而言具
           with an alternative use to the Group and the Group                  替代用途之資產，且本集團對迄今完
           has an enforceable right to payment for performance                 成之履約付款具有可執行權利。
           completed to date.


     If a performance obligation is satisfied over time, revenue         倘履約責任隨時間達成，收入參考達成該履
     is recognised by reference to the progress towards                  約責任之進度確認，否則收入於客戶取得產
     complete satisfaction of that performance obligation.               品或服務之控制權時確認。
     Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when
     the customer obtains control of the product or service.

     received.

     with the costs they are intended to compensate.

     the Group with no future related costs are recognised in               於損益內確認。
     profit or loss in the period in which they become

     deferred tax.

     year. Taxable profit differs from profit recognised in profit          應課稅溢利不包括其他年度之應課稅或應扣
     or loss because it excludes items of income or expense                 減之收入及開支項目，亦不包括可作免稅或
     that are taxable or deductible in other years and it further           不可作稅項扣減之項目，故與損益所確認溢

     Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates        期末前已制定或實質制定之稅率計算。
     that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the
     end of the reporting period.

     Impairment of assets                                                  資產減值

     Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life or not          具有無限可使用年期或尚未可供使用之無形
     yet available for use are reviewed annually for impairment            資產會每年進行減值檢討，並於發生任何事
     and are reviewed for impairment whenever events or                    件或情況有變顯示未必能夠收回賬面值時檢
     changes in circumstances indicate the carrying amount                 討是否減值。
     may not be recoverable.


     At the end of each reporting period, the Group reviews                於各報告期末，本集團會對其有形及其他無
     the carrying amounts of its tangible and other intangible             形資產（商譽、遞延稅項資產、存貨及應收
     assets except goodwill, deferred tax assets, inventories              款項除外）之賬面值進行檢討，以釐定有否
     and receivables to determine whether there is any                     任何跡象顯示該等資產出現減值虧損。倘出
     indication that those assets have suffered an impairment              現任有關跡象，則會估計該項資產之可收回
     loss. If any such indication exists, the recoverable amount           金額以釐定任何減值虧損之程度。倘未能估
     of the asset is estimated in order to determine the extent            計個別資產之可收回金額，則本集團會估計
     of any impairment loss. Where it is not possible to                   該項資產所屬之現金產生單位（「現金產生單
     estimate the recoverable amount of an individual asset,               位」）之可收回金額。
     the Group estimates the recoverable amount of cash-
     generating units (“CGU”) to which the asset belongs.


     Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of          可收回金額為公平值減銷售成本及其使用價
     disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the             值兩者中之較高者。評估使用價值時，估計
     estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present           未來現金流量以除稅前貼現率貼現至彼等之
     value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current             現值，而該貼現率反映當時市場對金錢時間
     market assessments of the time value of money and the                 價值之評估及該項資產之特有風險。
     risks specific to the asset.


     If the recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is estimated             倘資產或現金產生單位之可收回金額估計低
     to be less than its carrying amount, the carrying amount of           於其賬面值，則將該資產或現金產生單位之
     the asset or CGU is reduced to its recoverable amount. An             賬面值減低至其可收回金額。減值虧損會於
     impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss,          損益內即時確認，除非有關資產按重估價值
     unless the relevant asset is carried at a revalued amount,            列賬，在該情況下，減值虧損則視作重估減
     in which case the impairment loss is treated as a                     少處理。
     revaluation decrease.

     Impairment of assets (continued)                                       資產減值（續）

     Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the                    倘減值虧損於其後撥回，則將該資產或現金
     carrying amount of the asset or CGU is increased to the                產生單位之賬面值增加至其重新估算之可收
     revised estimate of its recoverable amount, but so that the            回金額，惟增加後之賬面值不能超過該資產
     increased carrying amount does not exceed the carrying                 或現金產生單位過往年度已確認為無減值虧
     amount that would have been determined (net of                         損之賬面值（扣除攤銷或折舊）。減值虧損之
     amortisation or depreciation) had no impairment loss been              撥回會於損益內即時確認，除非有關資產按
     recognised for the asset or CGU in prior years. A reversal             重估價值列賬，在該情況下，減值虧損撥回
     of an impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit              則視作重估增加處理。
     or loss, unless the relevant asset is carried at a revalued
     amount, in which case the reversal of the impairment loss
     is treated as a revaluation increase.


     Provisions and contingent liabilities                                  撥備及或然負債

     Provisions are recognised for liabilities of uncertain timing          倘本集團須就已發生之事件承擔現有法律或
     or amount when the Group has a present legal or                        推定責任，而且履行責任可能涉及經濟效益
     constructive obligation arising as a result of a past event, it        之流出，並可作出可靠之估計，則須就不肯
     is probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be               定時間或數額之負債確認撥備。倘金錢之時
     required to settle the obligation and a reliable estimate              間價值重大，則撥備將會以預計履行責任之
     can be made. Where the time value of money is material,                支出現值列示。
     provisions are stated at the present value of the
     expenditures expected to settle the obligation.


     Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                   倘不大可能涉及經濟效益之流出，或無法作
     benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                     出可靠之估計，則將責任披露為或然負債，
     estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                   惟經濟效益流出之可能性極低則除外。須視
     contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow is             乎某項或多項未來事件是否發生才能確定存
     remote. Possible obligations, whose existence will only be             在與否之可能責任，亦會披露為或然負債，
     confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or                惟經濟效益流出之可能性極低則除外。
     more future events are also disclosed as contingent
     liabilities unless the probability of outflow is remote.

     The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial            本集團之業務承受各種財務風險：外幣風
     risks: foreign currency risk, price risk, interest rate risk,         險、價格風險、利率風險、信貸風險及流動

     of financial markets and seeks to minimise potential                  本集團財務表現造成之潛在不利影響。
     adverse effects on the Group’s financial performance.

           The Group has minimal exposure to foreign currency                    由於本集團大部分業務交易、資產及
           risk as most of its business transactions, assets and                 負債主要以本集團實體之功能貨幣計

           functional currencies of the Group entities. The                      團在外幣交易、資產及負債方面，現
           Group currently does not have a foreign currency                      時沒有外幣對沖政策。本集團將密切

           transactions, assets and liabilities. The Group will                  對沖重大之外幣風險。
           monitor its foreign currency exposure closely and will
           consider hedging significant foreign currency

            approximate their respective fair values.


6.   FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                           6.   公平值計量

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an              公平值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序交易
     asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction        中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之價
     between market participants at the measurement date.                   格。以下公平值計量披露乃採用公平值架
     The following disclosures of fair value measurements use a             構，此架構把輸入數據分為三個估價層級用
     fair value hierarchy that categorises into three levels the            於量度公平值：
     inputs to valuation techniques used to measure fair value:


     Level 1 inputs: quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                   第 1 級輸入數據：本集團可於計量日獲取之
     markets for identical assets or liabilities that the Group can         相同資產或負債於活躍市場之報價（未經調
     access at the measurement date.                                        整）。


     Level 2 inputs: inputs other than quoted prices included               第 2 級輸入數據：就資產或負債可直接或間
     within level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability,         接觀察取得的除第 1 級之報價之外之輸入數
     either directly or indirectly.                                         據。


     Level 3 inputs: unobservable inputs for the asset or                   第 3 級輸入數據：資產或負債之不可觀察輸
     liability.                                                             入數據。


     The Group’s policy is to recognise transfers into and                  本集團之政策是於引起轉移之事件或情況變
     transfers out of any of the three levels as of the date of the         化發生之日確認任何三個等級之轉入或轉出。
     event or change in circumstances that caused the transfer.
